<content full_path="server/server.js">
import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import cors from 'cors';
import connectDB from './config/db.js';
import { notFound, errorHandler } from './middleware/error.js';
import authRoutes from './routes/authRoutes.js';
import studentRoutes from './routes/studentRoutes.js';
import teacherRoutes from './routes/teacherRoutes.js';
import subjectRoutes from './routes/subjectRoutes.js';
import classRoutes from './routes/classRoutes.js';
import registrationRoutes from './routes/registrationRoutes.js';
import gradeRoutes from './routes/gradeRoutes.js';

const app = express();
app.use(cors({ origin: process.env.CLIENT_URL }));
app.use(express.json());

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => res.json({ ok: true }));
app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/students', studentRoutes);
app.use('/api/teachers', teacherRoutes);
app.use('/api/subjects', subjectRoutes);
app.use('/api/classes', classRoutes);
app.use('/api/registrations', registrationRoutes);
app.use('/api/grades', gradeRoutes);

app.use(notFound);
app.use(errorHandler);

await connectDB();
app.listen(process.env.PORT || 5000, () => console.log(`API running on port ${process.env.PORT || 5000}`));

</content>

<content full_path="server/seed.js">
// npm run seed       -> creates the admin account only
// npm run seed:demo  -> also adds sample teachers, subjects, classes, students, registrations and grades
import 'dotenv/config';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import connectDB from './config/db.js';
import User from './models/User.js';
import Teacher from './models/Teacher.js';
import Subject from './models/Subject.js';
import Class from './models/Class.js';
import Registration from './models/Registration.js';
import Grade from './models/Grade.js';
import createStudent from './utils/createStudent.js';

await connectDB();

// ----- Admin -----
const email = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL.toLowerCase();
if (await User.findOne({ email })) {
  console.log('Admin already exists.');
} else {
  await User.create({ email, passwordHash: await bcrypt.hash(process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD, 10), role: 'admin' });
  console.log(`Admin created: ${email}`);
}

// ----- Demo data -----
if (process.argv.includes('--demo')) {
  if (await Subject.countDocuments()) {
    console.log('Demo data already exists. Skipped.');
  } else {
    const teachers = await Teacher.insertMany([
      { name: 'Prof. Smith', email: 'smith@national-u.edu.ph', department: 'Computing' },
      { name: 'Prof. Johnson', email: 'johnson@national-u.edu.ph', department: 'Information Security' },
      { name: 'Prof. Williams', email: 'williams@national-u.edu.ph', department: 'Data Systems' },
      { name: 'Prof. Brown', email: 'brown@national-u.edu.ph', department: 'Computing' },
      { name: 'Prof. Davis', email: 'davis@national-u.edu.ph', department: 'Networks' },
    ]);
    const subjects = await Subject.insertMany([
      { code: 'IT201', name: 'Web Application Development', units: 3 },
      { code: 'IT202', name: 'Information Assurance and Security', units: 3 },
      { code: 'IT203', name: 'Database Management Systems', units: 4 },
      { code: 'IT204', name: 'Data Structures and Algorithms', units: 3 },
      { code: 'IT302', name: 'Networking', units: 3 },
    ]);
    const T = Object.fromEntries(teachers.map((t) => [t.name, t._id]));
    const S = Object.fromEntries(subjects.map((s) => [s.code, s._id]));

    const classes = await Class.insertMany([
      { subjectId: S.IT201, teacherId: T['Prof. Smith'], section: 'BSIT-3A', day: 'Mon', start: '09:00', end: '10:30', room: 'Room 301' },
      { subjectId: S.IT203, teacherId: T['Prof. Williams'], section: 'BSIT-3A', day: 'Tue', start: '10:30', end: '12:00', room: 'Lab 2' },
      { subjectId: S.IT202, teacherId: T['Prof. Johnson'], section: 'BSIT-3A', day: 'Wed', start: '13:00', end: '14:30', room: 'Room 305' },
      { subjectId: S.IT204, teacherId: T['Prof. Brown'], section: 'BSIT-3A', day: 'Fri', start: '08:00', end: '09:30', room: 'Room 204' },
      { subjectId: S.IT302, teacherId: T['Prof. Davis'], section: 'BSIT-3A', day: 'Thu', start: '14:30', end: '16:00', room: 'Room 310' },
      { subjectId: S.IT201, teacherId: T['Prof. Smith'], section: 'BSIT-3B', day: 'Mon', start: '10:30', end: '12:00', room: 'Room 302' },
      { subjectId: S.IT204, teacherId: T['Prof. Brown'], section: 'BSIT-3B', day: 'Tue', start: '11:00', end: '12:30', room: 'Room 204' },
    ]);
    const C = Object.fromEntries(classes.map((c, i) => [`c${i + 1}`, c._id]));

    const bryce = await createStudent({ name: 'Bryce Amber', email: 'udaundobb@students.national-u.edu.ph', phone: '+639171234567', dob: '2004-05-05', course: 'BSIT - Web Development', password: 'student123' });
    const maria = await createStudent({ name: 'Maria Santos', email: 'msantos@students.national-u.edu.ph', phone: '+639181112233', dob: '2004-09-11', course: 'BSCS - Intelligent Systems' });
    const juan = await createStudent({ name: 'Juan Dela Cruz', email: 'jdelacruz@students.national-u.edu.ph', phone: '+639191234500', dob: '2003-12-20', course: 'BSIT - Network Security' });

    await Registration.insertMany([
      { studentId: bryce._id, classId: C.c1, status: 'approved' },
      { studentId: bryce._id, classId: C.c2, status: 'approved' },
      { studentId: bryce._id, classId: C.c3, status: 'approved' },
      { studentId: bryce._id, classId: C.c4, status: 'approved' },
      { studentId: bryce._id, classId: C.c5, status: 'pending' },
      { studentId: maria._id, classId: C.c6, status: 'pending' },
      { studentId: juan._id, classId: C.c1, status: 'approved' },
    ]);
    await Grade.insertMany([
      { studentId: bryce._id, subjectId: S.IT201, midterm: 1.5, finals: 1.25 },
      { studentId: bryce._id, subjectId: S.IT202, midterm: 1.75, finals: 1.5 },
      { studentId: bryce._id, subjectId: S.IT203, midterm: 1.25, finals: 1.25 },
      { studentId: bryce._id, subjectId: S.IT204, midterm: 2, finals: 1.75 },
    ]);
    console.log('Demo data added. Student login: udaundobb@students.national-u.edu.ph / student123');
  }
}

await mongoose.disconnect();

<content full_path="server/middleware/auth.js">
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import User from '../models/User.js';
import Student from '../models/Student.js';

export const protect = async (req, res, next) => {
  const header = req.headers.authorization || '';
  const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : null;
  if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ message: 'Please log in.' });
  try {
    const { id } = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
    const user = await User.findById(id).select('-passwordHash');
    if (!user) throw new Error('no user');
    req.user = user;
    if (user.role === 'student') req.student = await Student.findOne({ userId: user._id });
    next();
  } catch {
    res.status(401).json({ message: 'Session expired. Please log in again.' });
  }
};

export const adminOnly = (req, res, next) =>
  req.user.role === 'admin' ? next() : res.status(403).json({ message: 'Admins only.' });

export const studentOnly = (req, res, next) =>
  req.user.role === 'student' && req.student ? next() : res.status(403).json({ message: 'Students only.' });

</content>

<content full_path="server/middleware/error.js">
export const notFound = (req, res) => res.status(404).json({ message: 'Route not found.' });

export const errorHandler = (err, req, res, next) => { // eslint-disable-line no-unused-vars
  if (err.status) return res.status(err.status).json({ message: err.message });
  if (err.name === 'ValidationError')
    return res.status(400).json({ message: Object.values(err.errors).map((e) => e.message).join(' ') });
  if (err.code === 11000)
    return res.status(409).json({ message: `${Object.keys(err.keyPattern || {}).join(', ')} already exists.` });
  if (err.name === 'CastError') return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Invalid id.' });
  console.error(err);
  res.status(500).json({ message: 'Server error.' });
};

</content>

<content full_path="server/utils/schedule.js">
export const toMin = (t) => {
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
};

// Two classes overlap when they are on the same day and their times cross.
export const overlaps = (a, b) =>
  a.day === b.day && toMin(a.start) < toMin(b.end) && toMin(b.start) < toMin(a.end);

</content>

<content full_path="server/utils/createStudent.js">
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import User from '../models/User.js';
import Student from '../models/Student.js';
import HttpError from './httpError.js';
import { DEFAULT_PASSWORD } from './constants.js';

export const nextStudentNo = async () => {
  const last = await Student.findOne().sort({ studentNo: -1 }).select('studentNo');
  const n = last ? Number(last.studentNo.split('-')[1]) + 1 : 1;
  return `2026-${String(n).padStart(5, '0')}`;
};

// Makes the login (User) and the profile (Student) together.
export default async function createStudent({ name, email, phone, dob, course, password }) {
  const mail = String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(mail)) throw new HttpError(400, 'Enter a valid email address.');
  if (!name?.trim()) throw new HttpError(400, 'Name is required.');
  if (password && password.length < 8) throw new HttpError(400, 'Password must have at least 8 characters.');
  if (await User.exists({ email: mail })) throw new HttpError(409, 'That email already has an account.');

  const user = await User.create({
    email: mail,
    passwordHash: await bcrypt.hash(password || DEFAULT_PASSWORD, 10),
    role: 'student',
  });
  try {
    return await Student.create({
      userId: user._id,
      studentNo: await nextStudentNo(),
      name: name.trim(),
      phone: phone || undefined,
      dob: dob || undefined,
      course,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    await User.deleteOne({ _id: user._id }); // undo the login if the profile fails
    throw err;
  }
}

</content>

<content full_path="server/models/Grade.js">
import mongoose from 'mongoose';

const score = { type: Number, min: [1, 'Grades must be 1.00 to 5.00.'], max: [5, 'Grades must be 1.00 to 5.00.'], default: null };

const gradeSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    studentId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Student', required: true },
    subjectId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Subject', required: true },
    midterm: score,
    finals: score,
  },
  { toJSON: { virtuals: true }, toObject: { virtuals: true } }
);

gradeSchema.index({ studentId: 1, subjectId: 1 }, { unique: true });

// Computed, not stored: average of midterm and finals.
gradeSchema.virtual('finalRating').get(function () {
  return this.midterm == null || this.finals == null ? null : (this.midterm + this.finals) / 2;
});

// Computed, not stored: passed when 3.00 or better.
gradeSchema.virtual('remarks').get(function () {
  const r = this.finalRating;
  return r == null ? null : r <= 3 ? 'passed' : 'failed';
});

export default mongoose.model('Grade', gradeSchema);

</content>

<content full_path="server/models/Class.js">
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import { DAYS } from '../utils/constants.js';
import { toMin } from '../utils/schedule.js';

const time = { type: String, required: true, match: [/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/, 'Time must look like HH:MM.'] };

const classSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  subjectId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Subject', required: true },
  teacherId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Teacher', required: true },
  section: { type: String, required: [true, 'Section is required.'], trim: true },
  day: { type: String, enum: DAYS, required: true },
  start: time,
  end: time,
  room: { type: String, required: [true, 'Room is required.'], trim: true },
});

classSchema.pre('validate', function (next) {
  if (this.start && this.end && toMin(this.end) <= toMin(this.start))
    this.invalidate('end', 'End time must be after the start time.');
  next();
});

export default mongoose.model('Class', classSchema);

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/studentController.js">
import User from '../models/User.js';
import Student from '../models/Student.js';
import Registration from '../models/Registration.js';
import Grade from '../models/Grade.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';
import createStudent from '../utils/createStudent.js';

const withEmail = (s) => ({ ...s, email: s.userId?.email, userId: s.userId?._id });

// GET /api/students?q=maria
export const getStudents = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const q = req.query.q?.trim();
  const filter = q ? { $or: [{ name: new RegExp(q, 'i') }, { studentNo: new RegExp(q, 'i') }, { course: new RegExp(q, 'i') }] } : {};
  const list = await Student.find(filter).populate('userId', 'email').sort('studentNo').lean();
  res.json(list.map(withEmail));
});

// POST /api/students  (admin adds one student)
export const createStudentByAdmin = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.status(201).json(await createStudent(req.body));
});

// POST /api/students/bulk  body: { students: [...] }
export const bulkCreateStudents = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { students } = req.body;
  if (!Array.isArray(students) || !students.length) throw new HttpError(400, 'Send a list of students.');
  const created = [];
  const skipped = [];
  for (const [i, row] of students.entries()) {
    try {
      created.push(await createStudent(row));
    } catch (err) {
      skipped.push({ row: i + 1, reason: err.message });
    }
  }
  res.status(201).json({ created: created.length, skipped });
});

// PUT /api/students/:id  (admin edits)
export const updateStudent = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { name, phone, dob, course, email } = req.body;
  const student = await Student.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!student) throw new HttpError(404, 'Student not found.');
  if (email) {
    const mail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    if (await User.exists({ email: mail, _id: { $ne: student.userId } })) throw new HttpError(409, 'That email is already used.');
    await User.updateOne({ _id: student.userId }, { email: mail });
  }
  Object.assign(student, { name, phone: phone || undefined, dob: dob || undefined, course });
  await student.save();
  res.json(student);
});

// PUT /api/students/me  (student edits own profile)
export const updateMe = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { name, phone, dob } = req.body;
  Object.assign(req.student, { name, phone: phone || undefined, dob: dob || undefined });
  await req.student.save();
  res.json(req.student);
});

// DELETE /api/students/:id  (also removes login, registrations, grades)
export const deleteStudent = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const student = await Student.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!student) throw new HttpError(404, 'Student not found.');
  await Registration.deleteMany({ studentId: student._id });
  await Grade.deleteMany({ studentId: student._id });
  await User.deleteOne({ _id: student.userId });
  await student.deleteOne();
  res.json({ message: 'Student deleted.' });
});

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/classController.js">
import Class from '../models/Class.js';
import Subject from '../models/Subject.js';
import Teacher from '../models/Teacher.js';
import Registration from '../models/Registration.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';
import { overlaps } from '../utils/schedule.js';

const populate = (q) => q.populate('subjectId', 'code name units').populate('teacherId', 'name');

// Looks for a teacher, room or section that is already busy at this time.
const findConflict = async (data, ignoreId) => {
  const query = { day: data.day };
  if (ignoreId) query._id = { $ne: ignoreId };
  const same = await Class.find(query).populate('subjectId', 'code');
  for (const c of same) {
    if (!overlaps(c, data)) continue;
    const where = `${c.subjectId?.code} (${c.section}) on ${c.day} ${c.start}-${c.end}`;
    if (String(c.teacherId) === String(data.teacherId)) return `Teacher conflict: already teaches ${where}.`;
    if (c.room.toLowerCase() === data.room.toLowerCase()) return `Room conflict: ${c.room} is used by ${where}.`;
    if (c.section.toLowerCase() === data.section.toLowerCase()) return `Section conflict: ${data.section} already has ${where}.`;
  }
  return null;
};

const pick = (b) => ({
  subjectId: b.subjectId, teacherId: b.teacherId, section: b.section?.trim(),
  day: b.day, start: b.start, end: b.end, room: b.room?.trim(),
});

const check = async (data, ignoreId) => {
  if (!(await Subject.exists({ _id: data.subjectId }))) throw new HttpError(400, 'Subject not found.');
  if (!(await Teacher.exists({ _id: data.teacherId }))) throw new HttpError(400, 'Teacher not found.');
  const probe = new Class(data);
  await probe.validate(); // format and end > start
  const conflict = await findConflict(data, ignoreId);
  if (conflict) throw new HttpError(409, conflict);
};

// GET /api/classes
export const getClasses = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const classes = await populate(Class.find().sort('day start')).lean();
  const counts = await Registration.aggregate([
    { $match: { status: 'approved' } },
    { $group: { _id: '$classId', n: { $sum: 1 } } },
  ]);
  const map = Object.fromEntries(counts.map((c) => [String(c._id), c.n]));
  res.json(classes.map((c) => ({ ...c, enrolled: map[String(c._id)] || 0 })));
});

// POST /api/classes
export const createClass = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const data = pick(req.body);
  await check(data);
  res.status(201).json(await Class.create(data));
});

// PUT /api/classes/:id
export const updateClass = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const cls = await Class.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!cls) throw new HttpError(404, 'Class not found.');
  const data = pick(req.body);
  await check(data, cls._id);
  Object.assign(cls, data);
  await cls.save();
  res.json(cls);
});

// DELETE /api/classes/:id  (also removes its registrations)
export const deleteClass = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const cls = await Class.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!cls) throw new HttpError(404, 'Class not found.');
  await Registration.deleteMany({ classId: cls._id });
  await cls.deleteOne();
  res.json({ message: 'Class deleted.' });
});

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/subjectController.js">
import Subject from '../models/Subject.js';
import Class from '../models/Class.js';
import Registration from '../models/Registration.js';
import Grade from '../models/Grade.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';

// GET /api/subjects
export const getSubjects = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await Subject.find().sort('code'));
});

// POST /api/subjects
export const createSubject = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { code, name, units } = req.body;
  res.status(201).json(await Subject.create({ code, name, units }));
});

// POST /api/subjects/bulk  body: { subjects: [...] }
export const bulkCreateSubjects = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { subjects } = req.body;
  if (!Array.isArray(subjects) || !subjects.length) throw new HttpError(400, 'Send a list of subjects.');
  const skipped = [];
  let created = 0;
  for (const [i, s] of subjects.entries()) {
    try {
      await Subject.create({ code: s.code, name: s.name, units: Number(s.units) });
      created += 1;
    } catch (err) {
      skipped.push({ row: i + 1, reason: err.message });
    }
  }
  res.status(201).json({ created, skipped });
});

// PUT /api/subjects/:id
export const updateSubject = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { code, name, units } = req.body;
  const subject = await Subject.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!subject) throw new HttpError(404, 'Subject not found.');
  Object.assign(subject, { code, name, units });
  await subject.save();
  res.json(subject);
});

// DELETE /api/subjects/:id  (also removes its classes, registrations, grades)
export const deleteSubject = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const subject = await Subject.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!subject) throw new HttpError(404, 'Subject not found.');
  const classIds = (await Class.find({ subjectId: subject._id }).select('_id')).map((c) => c._id);
  await Registration.deleteMany({ classId: { $in: classIds } });
  await Class.deleteMany({ subjectId: subject._id });
  await Grade.deleteMany({ subjectId: subject._id });
  await subject.deleteOne();
  res.json({ message: 'Subject deleted.' });
});

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/authController.js">
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import User from '../models/User.js';
import Student from '../models/Student.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';
import createStudent from '../utils/createStudent.js';

const sign = (user) => jwt.sign({ id: user._id }, process.env.JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '7d' });

const payload = async (user) => ({
  user: { id: user._id, email: user.email, role: user.role },
  student: user.role === 'student' ? await Student.findOne({ userId: user._id }) : null,
});

// POST /api/auth/register  (student sign up)
export const register = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { name, email, course, password } = req.body;
  if (!password) throw new HttpError(400, 'Password is required.');
  const student = await createStudent({ name, email, course, password });
  const user = await User.findById(student.userId);
  res.status(201).json({ token: sign(user), ...(await payload(user)) });
});

// POST /api/auth/login
export const login = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { email, password } = req.body;
  const user = await User.findOne({ email: String(email || '').toLowerCase().trim() });
  if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password || '', user.passwordHash)))
    throw new HttpError(401, 'Wrong email or password. Please try again.');
  res.json({ token: sign(user), ...(await payload(user)) });
});

// GET /api/auth/me
export const me = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => res.json(await payload(req.user)));

// PUT /api/auth/password
export const changePassword = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { current, next } = req.body;
  const user = await User.findById(req.user._id);
  if (!(await bcrypt.compare(current || '', user.passwordHash))) throw new HttpError(400, 'Current password is wrong.');
  if (!next || next.length < 8) throw new HttpError(400, 'New password must have at least 8 characters.');
  if (next === current) throw new HttpError(400, 'New password must be different from the current one.');
  user.passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(next, 10);
  await user.save();
  res.json({ message: 'Password changed.' });
});

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/teacherController.js">
import Teacher from '../models/Teacher.js';

// POST /api/teachers/bulk  body: { teachers: [...] }

  if (!Array.isArray(teachers) || !teachers.length) throw new HttpError(400, 'Send a list of teachers.');
  const skipped = [];
  let created = 0;
  for (const [i, t] of teachers.entries()) {
    try {
      await Teacher.create({ name: t.name, email: t.email, department: t.department });
      created += 1;
    } catch (err) {
      skipped.push({ row: i + 1, reason: err.message });
    }
  }
  res.status(201).json({ created, skipped });

  if (!teacher) throw new HttpError(404, 'Teacher not found.');
  Object.assign(teacher, { name, email, department });
  await teacher.save();
  res.json(teacher);
});

// DELETE /api/teachers/:id  (blocked while the teacher still has classes)
export const deleteTeacher = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const n = await Class.countDocuments({ teacherId: req.params.id });
  if (n) throw new HttpError(400, `This teacher still teaches ${n} class(es). Reassign or delete those classes first.`);
  const deleted = await Teacher.findByIdAndDelete(req.params.id);
  if (!deleted) throw new HttpError(404, 'Teacher not found.');
  res.json({ message: 'Teacher deleted.' });
});

<content full_path="server/controllers/registrationController.js">
import Registration from '../models/Registration.js';
import Class from '../models/Class.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';
import { overlaps } from '../utils/schedule.js';
import { MAX_UNITS } from '../utils/constants.js';

const withClass = (q) =>
  q.populate({ path: 'classId', populate: [{ path: 'subjectId', select: 'code name units' }, { path: 'teacherId', select: 'name' }] });

// GET /api/registrations?status=pending   (admin)
export const getRegistrations = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const filter = req.query.status ? { status: req.query.status } : {};
  res.json(await withClass(Registration.find(filter).populate('studentId', 'name studentNo').sort('-createdAt')));
});

// GET /api/registrations/mine   (student)
export const getMyRegistrations = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await withClass(Registration.find({ studentId: req.student._id })));
});

// POST /api/registrations   body: { classId }   (student)
export const createRegistration = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const cls = await Class.findById(req.body.classId).populate('subjectId');
  if (!cls) throw new HttpError(404, 'Class not found.');

  const mine = await withClass(Registration.find({ studentId: req.student._id, status: { $ne: 'rejected' } }));

  if (mine.some((r) => String(r.classId.subjectId._id) === String(cls.subjectId._id)))
    throw new HttpError(400, 'You already registered for this subject.');

  const clash = mine.find((r) => overlaps(r.classId, cls));
  if (clash) throw new HttpError(400, `Overlaps with ${clash.classId.subjectId.code} on ${clash.classId.day}.`);

  const units = mine.reduce((n, r) => n + r.classId.subjectId.units, 0) + cls.subjectId.units;
  if (units > MAX_UNITS) throw new HttpError(400, `This goes over the ${MAX_UNITS}-unit limit.`);

  // If this class was rejected before, send it again as pending.
  const old = await Registration.findOne({ studentId: req.student._id, classId: cls._id });
  if (old) {
    old.status = 'pending';
    await old.save();
    return res.status(201).json(old);
  }
  res.status(201).json(await Registration.create({ studentId: req.student._id, classId: cls._id }));
});

// PUT /api/registrations/:id/status   body: { status }   (admin)
export const setStatus = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { status } = req.body;
  if (!['pending', 'approved', 'rejected'].includes(status)) throw new HttpError(400, 'Status is not valid.');
  const reg = await Registration.findById(req.params.id).populate('classId');
  if (!reg) throw new HttpError(404, 'Registration not found.');

  if (status === 'approved') {
    const approved = await Registration.find({ studentId: reg.studentId, status: 'approved', _id: { $ne: reg._id } }).populate('classId');
    const clash = approved.find((r) => overlaps(r.classId, reg.classId));
    if (clash) throw new HttpError(400, 'Cannot approve. It overlaps with another approved class.');
  }
  reg.status = status;
  await reg.save();
  res.json(reg);
});

// DELETE /api/registrations/:id   (student drops own, admin removes any)
export const deleteRegistration = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const reg = await Registration.findById(req.params.id);
  if (!reg) throw new HttpError(404, 'Registration not found.');
  if (req.user.role === 'student' && String(reg.studentId) !== String(req.student._id))
    throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only drop your own subjects.');
  await reg.deleteOne();
  res.json({ message: 'Registration removed.' });
});

</content>

<content full_path="server/controllers/gradeController.js">
import Grade from '../models/Grade.js';
import Class from '../models/Class.js';
import Registration from '../models/Registration.js';
import asyncHandler from '../utils/asyncHandler.js';
import HttpError from '../utils/httpError.js';

// GET /api/grades   (admin)
export const getGrades = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await Grade.find().populate('studentId', 'name studentNo').populate('subjectId', 'code name units'));
});

// GET /api/grades/mine   (student)
export const getMyGrades = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await Grade.find({ studentId: req.student._id }).populate('subjectId', 'code name units'));
});

// PUT /api/grades   body: { studentId, subjectId, midterm, finals }   (admin)
// Creates the grade if it is new, updates it if it already exists.
export const saveGrade = asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { studentId, subjectId } = req.body;
  const toScore = (v) => (v === '' || v == null ? null : Number(v));
  const midterm = toScore(req.body.midterm);
  const finals = toScore(req.body.finals);

  // Grades only make sense for subjects the student is approved in.
  const classIds = (await Class.find({ subjectId }).select('_id')).map((c) => c._id);
  const approved = await Registration.exists({ studentId, classId: { $in: classIds }, status: 'approved' });
  if (!approved) throw new HttpError(400, 'This student has no approved registration for that subject.');

  const grade = await Grade.findOneAndUpdate(
    { studentId, subjectId },
    { midterm, finals },
    { new: true, upsert: true, runValidators: true, setDefaultsOnInsert: true }
  );
  res.json(grade);
});

</content>

<content full_path="docs/database.md">
## Entities and attributes

### 1. users (for login)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| email | String | unique |
| passwordHash | String | never store the plain password |
| role | String | `admin` or `student` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 2. students

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| userId | ObjectId | links to users |
| studentNo | String | unique, like `2026-00123` |
| name | String |  |
| phone | String | optional |
| dob | Date | optional |
| course | String | like `BSIT - Web Development` |

### 3. teachers

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| name | String |  |
| email | String | unique |
| department | String |  |

### 4. subjects

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| code | String | unique, like `IT201` |
| name | String |  |
| units | Number | 1 to 6 |

### 5. classes (a subject offered in a section, with a schedule)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| teacherId | ObjectId | links to teachers |
| section | String | like `BSIT-3A` |
| day | String | `Mon` to `Sat` |
| start | String | `HH:MM` |
| end | String | `HH:MM` |
| room | String |  |

### 6. registrations (a student signs up for a class)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| classId | ObjectId | links to classes |
| status | String | `pending`, `approved`, or `rejected` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 7. grades

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| midterm | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |
| finals | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |

## Relations

- **users to students:** one to one. A student account has one user login.
- **subjects to classes:** one to many. One subject can have many classes (sections).
- **teachers to classes:** one to many. One teacher can teach many classes.
- **students to registrations:** one to many.
- **classes to registrations:** one to many.
- **students and classes:** many to many. The `registrations` collection sits in the middle.
- **students to grades:** one to many.
- **subjects to grades:** one to many.

## Rules to enforce

### Unique indexes

- `users.email`
- `students.studentNo`
- `teachers.email`
- `subjects.code`
- `registrations` on (`studentId`, `classId`)
- `grades` on (`studentId`, `subjectId`)

### Delete rules

- Delete a student: also delete their user, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a subject: also delete its classes, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a class: also delete its registrations.
- Delete a teacher: blocked if they still have classes.

### Checks in the backend

- Class end time must be after start time.
- No overlap for the same teacher, room, or section.
- Student cannot register for overlapping classes, the same subject twice, or more than 24 units.

## Do not store these (compute them)

- Final rating = average of midterm and finals.
- Remarks (passed or failed).
- GWA.
- Units loaded.
- Schedule conflicts.

Storing them can make the data wrong when something changes.

</content>

<content full_path="client/README.md">
# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.
