<content full_path="client/src/data.js">
// ---------- Constants ----------
// Mock accounts only. The real backend should hash passwords.
export const ADMIN = { email: 'admin@national-u.edu.ph', password: 'admin123', name: 'Administrator' };
export const DEMO_STUDENT = { email: 'udaundobb@students.national-u.edu.ph', password: 'student123' };
export const DEFAULT_PASSWORD = 'Welcome123';

export const DAYS = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
const DAY_NUM = { Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6 };
export const COURSES = [
  'BSIT - Web Development',
  'BSIT - Network Security',
  'BSCS - Intelligent Systems',
  'BSIS - Business Analytics',
];
export const MAX_UNITS = 24;

// ---------- Helpers ----------
export const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);

export const toMin = (t) => {
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
};

export const fmtTime = (t) => {
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  return `${((h + 11) % 12) + 1}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')} ${h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM'}`;
};

export const timeRange = (c) => `${fmtTime(c.start)} - ${fmtTime(c.end)}`;

export const fmtDate = (iso) =>
  iso
    ? new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-PH', { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })
    : '—';

// Two classes overlap when they are on the same day and the times cross.
export const overlaps = (a, b) =>
  a.day === b.day && toMin(a.start) < toMin(b.end) && toMin(b.start) < toMin(a.end);

export const daysFromToday = (day) => (DAY_NUM[day] - new Date().getDay() + 7) % 7;

export const initials = (name = '') =>
  name.split(' ').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).map((w) => w[0].toUpperCase()).join('');

export const pct = (n, d) => (d ? Math.round((n / d) * 100) : 0);

export const nextStudentNo = (students) => {
  const max = students.reduce((m, s) => Math.max(m, Number(s.studentNo.split('-')[1]) || 0), 0);
  return `2026-${String(max + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`;
};

export const enrich = (db, c) =>
  c && {
    ...c,
    subject: db.subjects.find((s) => s.id === c.subjectId),
    teacher: db.teachers.find((t) => t.id === c.teacherId),
  };

// Grades use the Philippine scale: 1.00 (best) to 5.00. Passing is 3.00 or better.
export const gradeOf = (grades, studentId, subjectId) =>
  grades.find((g) => g.studentId === studentId && g.subjectId === subjectId);

export const finalRating = (g) =>
  g && g.midterm !== '' && g.finals !== '' ? (Number(g.midterm) + Number(g.finals)) / 2 : null;

export const remarkOf = (rating) => (rating == null ? null : rating <= 3 ? 'passed' : 'failed');

</content>

<content full_path="client/src/App.jsx">
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useDB, buildSession } from './store';
import { api, getToken, setToken, setAuthFailHandler } from './api';
import Login from './Login';
import AdminPortal from './AdminPortal';
import StudentPortal from './StudentPortal';

export default function App() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null); // { role, user, student?, id? }
  const [booting, setBooting] = useState(() => !!getToken());
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    try { return localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light'; } catch { return 'light'; }
  });
  const db = useDB(session);

  const notify = useCallback((message, type = 'success') => setToast({ message, type, key: Date.now() }), []);
  const toggleTheme = () => setTheme((t) => (t === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'));
  const logout = () => { setToken(null); setSession(null); };

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
    try { localStorage.setItem('theme', theme); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, [theme]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return undefined;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [toast]);

  // If the login token expires, go back to the login page.
  useEffect(() => {
    setAuthFailHandler(() => {
      setSession(null);
      notify('Your session ended. Please log in again.', 'error');
    });
  }, [notify]);

  // Keep the user logged in after a page refresh.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!getToken()) return;
    api.get('/auth/me')
      .then((res) => setSession(buildSession(res)))
      .catch(() => setToken(null))
      .finally(() => setBooting(false));
  }, []);

  const student = session?.role === 'student' ? db.students.find((s) => s.id === session.id) : null;

  let screen;
  if (booting || (session && db.loading)) {
    screen = <div className="boot-screen">Loading...</div>;
  } else if (session && db.loadError) {
    screen = (
      <div className="boot-screen">
        <p>{db.loadError}</p>
        <div className="action-buttons" style={{ marginTop: '1rem' }}>
          <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => db.reload().catch((e) => notify(e.message, 'error'))}>Try again</button>
          <button className="btn-cancel" onClick={logout}>Log out</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  } else if (!session || (session.role === 'student' && !student)) {
    screen = <Login theme={theme} toggleTheme={toggleTheme} onLogin={setSession} notify={notify} />;
  } else if (session.role === 'admin') {
    screen = <AdminPortal db={db} theme={theme} toggleTheme={toggleTheme} onLogout={logout} notify={notify} />;
  } else {
    screen = <StudentPortal student={student} db={db} theme={theme} toggleTheme={toggleTheme} onLogout={logout} notify={notify} />;
  }

  return (
    <>
      {screen}
      {toast && <div key={toast.key} className={`toast toast-${toast.type}`} role="status">{toast.message}</div>}
    </>
  );
}

</content>

<content full_path="client/src/api.js">
// Small helper for talking to the Express server.
const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:5000/api';
const KEY = 'attendx_token';

export const getToken = () => {
  try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY); } catch { return null; }
};
export const setToken = (t) => {
  try { if (t) localStorage.setItem(KEY, t); else localStorage.removeItem(KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
};

// App.jsx sets this so an expired login sends the user back to the login page.
let onAuthFail = () => {};
export const setAuthFailHandler = (fn) => { onAuthFail = fn; };

async function request(method, path, body) {
  const token = getToken();
  let res;
  try {
    res = await fetch(BASE + path, {
      method,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}) },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Cannot reach the server. Is it running?');
  }
  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.status === 401 && path !== '/auth/login') { setToken(null); onAuthFail(); }
    const err = new Error(data.message || 'Something went wrong.');
    err.status = res.status;
    throw err;
  }
  return data;
}

export const api = {
  get: (p) => request('GET', p),
  post: (p, b) => request('POST', p, b),
  put: (p, b) => request('PUT', p, b),
  del: (p) => request('DELETE', p),
};

</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/database.md">
## Entities and attributes

### 1. users (for login)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| email | String | unique |
| passwordHash | String | never store the plain password |
| role | String | `admin` or `student` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 2. students

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| userId | ObjectId | links to users |
| studentNo | String | unique, like `2026-00123` |
| name | String |  |
| phone | String | optional |
| dob | Date | optional |
| course | String | like `BSIT - Web Development` |

### 3. teachers

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| name | String |  |
| email | String | unique |
| department | String |  |

### 4. subjects

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| code | String | unique, like `IT201` |
| name | String |  |
| units | Number | 1 to 6 |

### 5. classes (a subject offered in a section, with a schedule)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| teacherId | ObjectId | links to teachers |
| section | String | like `BSIT-3A` |
| day | String | `Mon` to `Sat` |
| start | String | `HH:MM` |
| end | String | `HH:MM` |
| room | String |  |

### 6. registrations (a student signs up for a class)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| classId | ObjectId | links to classes |
| status | String | `pending`, `approved`, or `rejected` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 7. grades

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| midterm | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |
| finals | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |

## Relations

- **users to students:** one to one. A student account has one user login.
- **subjects to classes:** one to many. One subject can have many classes (sections).
- **teachers to classes:** one to many. One teacher can teach many classes.
- **students to registrations:** one to many.
- **classes to registrations:** one to many.
- **students and classes:** many to many. The `registrations` collection sits in the middle.
- **students to grades:** one to many.
- **subjects to grades:** one to many.

## Rules to enforce

### Unique indexes

- `users.email`
- `students.studentNo`
- `teachers.email`
- `subjects.code`
- `registrations` on (`studentId`, `classId`)
- `grades` on (`studentId`, `subjectId`)

### Delete rules

- Delete a student: also delete their user, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a subject: also delete its classes, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a class: also delete its registrations.
- Delete a teacher: blocked if they still have classes.

### Checks in the backend

- Class end time must be after start time.
- No overlap for the same teacher, room, or section.
- Student cannot register for overlapping classes, the same subject twice, or more than 24 units.

## Do not store these (compute them)

- Final rating = average of midterm and finals.
- Remarks (passed or failed).
- GWA.
- Units loaded.
- Schedule conflicts.

Storing them can make the data wrong when something changes.

</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/server/models/Student.js">
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const studentSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  name: { 
    type: String, 
    required: true,
    trim: true 
  },
  roll: { 
    type: String, 
    required: true,
    unique: true 
  },
  phone: { 
    type: String, 
    required: true,
    match: [/^\+?[0-9]{10,15}$/, 'Please enter a valid phone number']
  },
  dob: { 
    type: String, // Kept as String to match your frontend 'DD-MM-YYYY' format for now
    required: true 
  },
  // This acts similarly to a foreign key, referencing the Class model
  classId: { 
    type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, 
    ref: 'Class',
    required: false // Set to false initially so you can insert data without a class
  }
}, { timestamps: true });

module.exports = mongoose.model('Student', studentSchema);
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/server/models/Class.js">
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const classSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  className: { 
    type: String, 
    required: true, 
    trim: true 
  },
  section: { 
    type: String, 
    required: true 
  },
  roomNo: { 
    type: String 
  }
}, { timestamps: true });

module.exports = mongoose.model('Class', classSchema);
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/server/docs/database.md">
## Entities and attributes

### 1. users (for login)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| email | String | unique |
| passwordHash | String | never store the plain password |
| role | String | `admin` or `student` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 2. students

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| userId | ObjectId | links to users |
| studentNo | String | unique, like `2026-00123` |
| name | String |  |
| phone | String | optional |
| dob | Date | optional |
| course | String | like `BSIT - Web Development` |

### 3. teachers

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| name | String |  |
| email | String | unique |
| department | String |  |

### 4. subjects

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| code | String | unique, like `IT201` |
| name | String |  |
| units | Number | 1 to 6 |

### 5. classes (a subject offered in a section, with a schedule)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| teacherId | ObjectId | links to teachers |
| section | String | like `BSIT-3A` |
| day | String | `Mon` to `Sat` |
| start | String | `HH:MM` |
| end | String | `HH:MM` |
| room | String |  |

### 6. registrations (a student signs up for a class)

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| classId | ObjectId | links to classes |
| status | String | `pending`, `approved`, or `rejected` |
| createdAt | Date |  |

### 7. grades

| Field | Type | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| \_id | ObjectId | primary key |
| studentId | ObjectId | links to students |
| subjectId | ObjectId | links to subjects |
| midterm | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |
| finals | Number | 1.00 to 5.00, optional |

## Relations

- **users to students:** one to one. A student account has one user login.
- **subjects to classes:** one to many. One subject can have many classes (sections).
- **teachers to classes:** one to many. One teacher can teach many classes.
- **students to registrations:** one to many.
- **classes to registrations:** one to many.
- **students and classes:** many to many. The `registrations` collection sits in the middle.
- **students to grades:** one to many.
- **subjects to grades:** one to many.

## Rules to enforce

### Unique indexes

- `users.email`
- `students.studentNo`
- `teachers.email`
- `subjects.code`
- `registrations` on (`studentId`, `classId`)
- `grades` on (`studentId`, `subjectId`)

### Delete rules

- Delete a student: also delete their user, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a subject: also delete its classes, registrations, and grades.
- Delete a class: also delete its registrations.
- Delete a teacher: blocked if they still have classes.

### Checks in the backend

- Class end time must be after start time.
- No overlap for the same teacher, room, or section.
- Student cannot register for overlapping classes, the same subject twice, or more than 24 units.

## Do not store these (compute them)

- Final rating = average of midterm and finals.
- Remarks (passed or failed).
- GWA.
- Units loaded.
- Schedule conflicts.

Storing them can make the data wrong when something changes.

</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/README.md">
# React + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend using TypeScript with type-aware lint rules enabled. Check out the [TS template](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/tree/main/packages/create-vite/template-react-ts) for information on how to integrate TypeScript and [`typescript-eslint`](https://typescript-eslint.io) in your project.

</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/src/components.jsx">
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { X, LogOut, Moon, Sun, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose();
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="modal-content" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title}>
        <div className="modal-header">
          <h2>{title}</h2>
          <button type="button" className="close-btn" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close"><X size={20} /></button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function ConfirmModal({ title, message, confirmLabel = 'Delete', danger = true, onConfirm, onClose }) {
  return (
    <Modal title={title} onClose={onClose}>
      <div>{message}</div>
      <div className="modal-actions">
        <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button type="button" className={danger ? 'btn-danger' : 'btn-primary'} onClick={onConfirm}>{confirmLabel}</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// field = { name, label, type, options, optional, placeholder, hint, min, max, step }
export function Field({ field, value, onChange }) {
  const id = `field-${field.name}`;
  const common = { id, name: field.name, value: value ?? '', onChange, required: !field.optional };
  return (
    <div className="form-group">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{field.label}{field.optional && ' (optional)'}</label>
      {field.type === 'select' ? (
        <select {...common}>
          <option value="" disabled>Select...</option>
          {field.options.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
        </select>
      ) : (
        <input
          {...common}
          type={field.type || 'text'}
          placeholder={field.placeholder}
          min={field.min}
          max={field.max}
          step={field.step}
          autoComplete={field.autoComplete}
        />
      )}
      {field.hint && <small className="form-hint">{field.hint}</small>}
    </div>
  );
}

const TONE = { pending: 'yellow', approved: 'green', rejected: 'red', passed: 'green', failed: 'red' };

export function Badge({ status, children }) {
  const label = children ?? status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1);
  return <span className={`stat-badge badge-${TONE[status] || 'blue'}`}>{label}</span>;
}

export function EmptyState({ title, text, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="empty-state">
      {title && <h3>{title}</h3>}
      <p>{text}</p>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, children }) {
  return (
    <header className="page-header page-header-row">
      <div>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p className="page-subtitle">{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      {children && <div className="no-print">{children}</div>}
    </header>
  );
}

// One layout for both portals: sidebar + main area.
// sections = [{ label?, items: [{ key, label, icon, count? }] }]
export function Shell({ brand, userLabel, sections, active, onNav, theme, toggleTheme, onLogout, children }) {
  const [closed, setClosed] = useState({});

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-layout">
      <aside className="sidebar">
        <div className="sidebar-header">
          <h2>{brand}</h2>
          <p className="sidebar-user">{userLabel}</p>
        </div>
        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {sections.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i}>
              {s.label && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="nav-group-header"
                  aria-expanded={!closed[s.label]}
                  onClick={() => setClosed((c) => ({ ...c, [s.label]: !c[s.label] }))}
                >
                  <span>{s.label}</span>
                  <ChevronDown size={16} style={{ transform: closed[s.label] ? 'rotate(-90deg)' : 'none' }} />
                </button>
              )}
              {!closed[s.label] &&
                s.items.map((it) => {
                  const Icon = it.icon;
                  return (
                    <a
                      key={it.key}
                      href="#"
                      className={`nav-item ${active === it.key ? 'active' : ''}`}
                      onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onNav(it.key); }}
                    >
                      <Icon size={18} /> {it.label}
                      {it.count > 0 && <span className="nav-count">{it.count}</span>}
                    </a>
                  );
                })}
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="sidebar-footer">
          <button className="theme-toggle-btn" onClick={toggleTheme}>
            {theme === 'light' ? <><Moon size={18} /> Dark Mode</> : <><Sun size={18} /> Light Mode</>}
          </button>
          <button className="logout-btn" onClick={onLogout}><LogOut size={18} /> Logout</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main className="main-content">{children}</main>
    </div>
  );
}
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/src/CrudPage.jsx">
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Search, Plus, Upload, Edit2, Trash2, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Modal, ConfirmModal, Field, EmptyState } from './components';

const PAGE_SIZE = 8;

/*
  One page for Add / Edit / Delete / Search / Bulk import.
  Props:
    noun        "Student"
    rows        list of records
    columns     [{ label, render(row) }]
    fields      form fields (see Field in components.jsx)
    searchText  (row) => string to search in
    onAdd(values), onEdit(id, values), onDelete(id)
    validate    (values, editingRow, batch) => error string or null
    canDelete   (row) => string (reason it is blocked) or null
    deleteNote  (row) => extra warning text
    nameOf      (row) => name shown in the delete message
    bulk        { fields: [names in CSV order], hint, defaults, onImport(list) }
*/
export default function CrudPage({
  noun, rows, columns, fields, searchText, onAdd, onEdit, onDelete,
  validate, canDelete, deleteNote, nameOf = (r) => r.name, bulk, notify,
}) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null); // { type: 'add' | 'edit' | 'delete' | 'bulk', row }
  const [form, setForm] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [bulkText, setBulkText] = useState('');

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = q ? rows.filter((r) => searchText(r).toLowerCase().includes(q)) : rows;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
  const current = Math.min(page, pages);
  const start = (current - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
  const visible = filtered.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE);

  const blank = () => Object.fromEntries(fields.map((f) => [f.name, f.default ?? '']));
  const clean = (values) =>
    Object.fromEntries(
      fields.map((f) => {
        let v = values[f.name] ?? '';
        if (typeof v === 'string') v = v.trim();
        if (f.type === 'number' && v !== '') v = Number(v);
        return [f.name, v];
      })
    );

  const open = (type, row = null) => {
    setError('');
    setBulkText('');
    setForm(type === 'edit' ? { ...row } : blank());
    setModal({ type, row });
  };
  const close = () => setModal(null);
  const change = (e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const values = clean(form);
    const err = validate?.(values, modal.row, []);
    if (err) return setError(err);
    if (modal.type === 'add') onAdd(values);
    else onEdit(modal.row.id, values);
    notify(`${noun} ${modal.type === 'add' ? 'added' : 'updated'}.`);
    close();
  };

  const readFile = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => setBulkText(String(reader.result));
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  const importBulk = () => {
    const header = fields.find((f) => f.name === bulk.fields[0])?.label.toLowerCase();
    const lines = bulkText.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const good = [];
    let bad = 0;
    lines.forEach((line, i) => {
      const cells = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim());
      if (i === 0 && cells[0].toLowerCase() === header) return; // skip header row
      const raw = { ...blank(), ...(bulk.defaults || {}) };
      bulk.fields.forEach((name, k) => { raw[name] = cells[k] || ''; });
      const values = clean(raw);
      const missing = fields.some((f) => !f.optional && values[f.name] === '');
      if (missing || validate?.(values, null, good)) { bad += 1; return; }
      good.push(values);
    });
    if (!good.length) return setError('No valid rows found. Check the format and try again.');
    bulk.onImport(good);
    notify(`Imported ${good.length} ${noun.toLowerCase()}(s)${bad ? `, skipped ${bad} invalid row(s)` : ''}.`);
    close();
  };

  const confirmDelete = () => {
    onDelete(modal.row.id);
    notify(`${noun} deleted.`);
    close();
  };

  const blocked = modal?.type === 'delete' ? canDelete?.(modal.row) : null;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar no-print">
        <div className="search-bar">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder={`Search ${noun.toLowerCase()}s...`}
            aria-label={`Search ${noun.toLowerCase()}s`}
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => { setQuery(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
          />
          <div className="btn-icon"><Search size={18} /></div>
        </div>
        <div className="action-buttons">
          <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => open('add')}><Plus size={18} /> Add New</button>
          {bulk && <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => open('bulk')}><Upload size={18} /> Add Multiple</button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="table-container">
        <div className="table-scroll">
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th>
                {columns.map((c) => <th key={c.label}>{c.label}</th>)}
                <th>Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {visible.map((row, i) => (
                <tr key={row.id}>
                  <td>{start + i + 1}</td>
                  {columns.map((c) => <td key={c.label}>{c.render(row)}</td>)}
                  <td>
                    <div className="actions-cell">
                      <button className="action-edit" title="Edit" aria-label={`Edit ${nameOf(row)}`} onClick={() => open('edit', row)}><Edit2 size={16} /></button>
                      <button className="action-delete" title="Delete" aria-label={`Delete ${nameOf(row)}`} onClick={() => open('delete', row)}><Trash2 size={16} /></button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          {visible.length === 0 && (
            <EmptyState
              text={q ? 'No results. Try a different search.' : `No ${noun.toLowerCase()}s yet. Click "Add New" to create one.`}
            />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="table-footer">
          <span>
            {filtered.length ? `Showing ${start + 1}-${start + visible.length} of ${filtered.length}` : '0 results'}
          </span>
          <div className="pager">
            <button className="action-edit" aria-label="Previous page" disabled={current === 1} onClick={() => setPage(current - 1)}><ChevronLeft size={16} /></button>
            <span>Page {current} of {pages}</span>
            <button className="action-edit" aria-label="Next page" disabled={current === pages} onClick={() => setPage(current + 1)}><ChevronRight size={16} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {(modal?.type === 'add' || modal?.type === 'edit') && (
        <Modal title={`${modal.type === 'add' ? 'Add New' : 'Edit'} ${noun}`} onClose={close}>
          <form onSubmit={submit}>
            {error && <div className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
            {fields.map((f) => <Field key={f.name} field={f} value={form[f.name]} onChange={change} />)}
            <div className="modal-actions">
              <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={close}>Cancel</button>
              <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save {noun}</button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {modal?.type === 'bulk' && (
        <Modal title={`Add Multiple ${noun}s`} onClose={close}>
          {error && <div className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          <p style={{ marginBottom: '0.75rem' }}>Upload a CSV file or paste one {noun.toLowerCase()} per line.</p>
          <p className="form-hint" style={{ marginBottom: '0.75rem' }}>Order: {bulk.hint}</p>
          <input type="file" accept=".csv,.txt" onChange={readFile} style={{ marginBottom: '0.75rem' }} />
          <textarea
            className="bulk-textarea"
            rows="6"
            placeholder={bulk.hint}
            value={bulkText}
            onChange={(e) => setBulkText(e.target.value)}
          />
          {bulk.note && <p className="form-hint">{bulk.note}</p>}
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={close}>Cancel</button>
            <button type="button" className="btn-primary" onClick={importBulk} disabled={!bulkText.trim()}>Import Data</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {modal?.type === 'delete' && blocked && (
        <Modal title="Cannot Delete" onClose={close}>
          <p>{blocked}</p>
          <div className="modal-actions"><button type="button" className="btn-primary" onClick={close}>OK</button></div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {modal?.type === 'delete' && !blocked && (
        <ConfirmModal
          title="Confirm Delete"
          message={
            <p>
              Are you sure you want to delete <strong>{nameOf(modal.row)}</strong>? This action cannot be undone.
              {deleteNote?.(modal.row) && <> {deleteNote(modal.row)}</>}
            </p>
          }
          onConfirm={confirmDelete}
          onClose={close}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/src/StudentPortal.jsx">
import { useState } from 'react';
import { LayoutDashboard, User, Book, Calendar, FileText, Printer, AlertTriangle, Lock, Edit2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Shell, PageHeader, Badge, Modal, ConfirmModal, Field, EmptyState } from './components';
import {
  DAYS, MAX_UNITS, enrich, overlaps, timeRange, toMin, fmtTime, fmtDate, gradeOf, finalRating, remarkOf,
  daysFromToday, initials, pct,
} from './data';

const NAV = [
  { key: 'dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { key: 'profile', label: 'Student Profile', icon: User },
  { key: 'subjects', label: 'Subject List', icon: Book },
  { key: 'schedule', label: 'Class Schedule', icon: Calendar },
  { key: 'records', label: 'Academic Records', icon: FileText },
];

export default function StudentPortal({ student, db, theme, toggleTheme, onLogout, notify }) {
  const [page, setPage] = useState('dashboard');
  const [dropTarget, setDropTarget] = useState(null);
  const [profileModal, setProfileModal] = useState(null); // 'edit' | 'password'

  // ----- Data for this student -----
  const regs = db.registrations
    .filter((r) => r.studentId === student.id)
    .map((r) => ({ ...r, cls: enrich(db, db.classes.find((c) => c.id === r.classId)) }))
    .filter((r) => r.cls?.subject);
  const active = regs.filter((r) => r.status !== 'rejected');
  const approved = regs.filter((r) => r.status === 'approved');
  const pending = regs.filter((r) => r.status === 'pending');
  const unitsLoaded = active.reduce((n, r) => n + r.cls.subject.units, 0);

  const conflicts = [];
  active.forEach((a, i) => active.slice(i + 1).forEach((b) => overlaps(a.cls, b.cls) && conflicts.push([a, b])));
  const conflictIds = new Set(conflicts.flat().map((r) => r.id));

  const rated = approved.map((r) => ({ r, rating: finalRating(gradeOf(db.grades, student.id, r.cls.subjectId)) }));
  const posted = rated.filter((x) => x.rating != null);
  const unitsPosted = posted.reduce((n, x) => n + x.r.cls.subject.units, 0);
  const gwa = unitsPosted ? posted.reduce((n, x) => n + x.rating * x.r.cls.subject.units, 0) / unitsPosted : null;

  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  const greeting = hour < 12 ? 'Good Morning' : hour < 18 ? 'Good Afternoon' : 'Good Evening';

  // ----- Registering for a class -----
  const blockReason = (c) => {
    if (active.some((r) => r.cls.subjectId === c.subjectId)) return 'Already registered for this subject.';
    const clash = active.find((r) => overlaps(r.cls, c));
    if (clash) return `Overlaps with ${clash.cls.subject.code} on ${clash.cls.day}.`;
    if (unitsLoaded + c.subject.units > MAX_UNITS) return `Over the ${MAX_UNITS}-unit limit.`;
    return null;
  };

  const register = (c) => {
    const reason = blockReason(c);
    if (reason) return notify(reason, 'error');
    db.register(student.id, c.id);
    notify('Registration sent. Wait for admin approval.');
  };

  const confirmDrop = () => {
    db.dropReg(dropTarget.id);
    notify(`${dropTarget.cls.subject.code} removed from your list.`);
    setDropTarget(null);
  };

  const available = db.classes.map((c) => enrich(db, c)).filter((c) => c.subject && !active.some((r) => r.classId === c.id));

  // ----- Pages -----
  const renderContent = () => {
    switch (page) {
      case 'dashboard': {
        const upcoming = approved
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => daysFromToday(a.cls.day) - daysFromToday(b.cls.day) || toMin(a.cls.start) - toMin(b.cls.start))
          .slice(0, 4);
        return (
          <>
            <PageHeader title={`${greeting}, ${student.name.split(' ')[0]}`} subtitle={student.course} />

            <div className="stats-grid">
              <div className="stat-card">
                <p className="stat-label">Registered Courses</p>
                <div className="stat-value">{approved.length} <Badge status="approved">Approved</Badge></div>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-card">
                <p className="stat-label">Weekly Classes</p>
                <div className="stat-value">{approved.length} <span className="stat-badge badge-blue">This week</span></div>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-card clickable" role="button" tabIndex={0} onClick={() => setPage('subjects')} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && setPage('subjects')}>
                <p className="stat-label">Pending Registrations</p>
                <div className="stat-value">{pending.length} <Badge status={pending.length ? 'pending' : 'approved'}>{pending.length ? 'Awaiting approval' : 'All clear'}</Badge></div>
              </div>
              <div className="stat-card clickable" role="button" tabIndex={0} onClick={() => setPage('schedule')} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && setPage('schedule')}>
                <p className="stat-label">Schedule Conflicts</p>
                <div className="stat-value">{conflicts.length} <Badge status={conflicts.length ? 'rejected' : 'approved'}>{conflicts.length ? 'Resolve now' : 'No conflicts'}</Badge></div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="dashboard-columns">
              <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '1.5rem' }}>
                <div className="card-title-row">
                  <h3>Upcoming Classes</h3>
                  <a href="#" className="link-accent" onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPage('schedule'); }}>Full Schedule</a>
                </div>
                {upcoming.length === 0 ? (
                  <EmptyState text="No approved classes yet.">
                    <button className="btn-primary btn-sm" style={{ margin: '1rem auto 0' }} onClick={() => setPage('subjects')}>Register for classes</button>
                  </EmptyState>
                ) : (
                  <div className="table-scroll">
                    <table>
                      <thead><tr><th>Day</th><th>Subject</th><th>Time</th><th>Room</th></tr></thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {upcoming.map((r) => (
                          <tr key={r.id}>
                            <td><strong>{r.cls.day}</strong></td>
                            <td>{r.cls.subject.name}<br /><small className="muted">{r.cls.subject.code}</small></td>
                            <td>{timeRange(r.cls)}</td>
                            <td>{r.cls.room}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '1.5rem' }}>
                <h3 style={{ marginBottom: '1.5rem' }}>Semester Progress</h3>
                <Progress label="Units Loaded" value={`${unitsLoaded}/${MAX_UNITS}`} width={pct(unitsLoaded, MAX_UNITS)} fill="fill-blue" />
                <Progress label="Registrations Approved" value={`${approved.length}/${active.length}`} width={pct(approved.length, active.length)} fill="fill-navy" />
                <Progress label="Grades Posted" value={`${posted.length}/${approved.length}`} width={pct(posted.length, approved.length)} fill="fill-green" />
                <div style={{ marginTop: '2rem' }}>
                  <p className="muted" style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem' }}>Current GWA</p>
                  <h2 style={{ fontSize: '2rem' }}>{gwa == null ? '—' : gwa.toFixed(2)}</h2>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        );
      }

      case 'profile':
        return (
          <>
            <PageHeader title="Student Profile">
              <div className="action-buttons">
                <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => setProfileModal('edit')}><Edit2 size={16} /> Edit Profile</button>
                <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => setProfileModal('password')}><Lock size={16} /> Password</button>
              </div>
            </PageHeader>
            <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '2rem' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '1.5rem', marginBottom: '2.5rem', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <div className="avatar-large">{initials(student.name)}</div>
                <div>
                  <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem' }}>{student.name}</h3>
                  <p className="muted">{student.course.split(' - ')[0]}</p>
                  <p className="muted">Specialization: {student.course.split(' - ')[1]}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="info-grid">
                <Info label="Email Address" value={student.email} />
                <Info label="Student Number" value={student.studentNo} />
                <Info label="Phone" value={student.phone || '—'} />
                <Info label="Date of Birth" value={fmtDate(student.dob)} />
                <Info label="Units Loaded" value={`${unitsLoaded} of ${MAX_UNITS}`} />
                <Info label="Registered Subjects" value={approved.length} />
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        );

      case 'subjects':
        return (
          <>
            <PageHeader title="Subject List" subtitle={`${unitsLoaded} of ${MAX_UNITS} units loaded`} />

            <h3 className="section-title">My Subjects</h3>
            {regs.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="table-container"><EmptyState text="You have no subjects yet. Register for a class below." /></div>
            ) : (
              <div className="subject-grid">
                {regs.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} className="subject-card">
                    <div className="card-title-row">
                      <h4 style={{ color: 'var(--btn-primary-bg)' }}>{r.cls.subject.name}</h4>
                      <Badge status={r.status} />
                    </div>
                    <p className="muted" style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', marginBottom: '1rem' }}>
                      {r.cls.subject.code} - {r.cls.section} - {r.cls.day} {timeRange(r.cls)}
                    </p>
                    <div className="card-title-row muted" style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem' }}>
                      <span>{r.cls.teacher?.name}</span>
                      <span>{r.cls.subject.units} Units</span>
                    </div>
                    <button className="btn-danger btn-sm" style={{ marginTop: '1rem' }} onClick={() => setDropTarget(r)}>
                      {r.status === 'rejected' ? 'Remove' : 'Drop'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            <h3 className="section-title">Available Classes</h3>
            <div className="table-container">
              <div className="table-scroll">
                <table>
                  <thead><tr><th>Subject</th><th>Section</th><th>Teacher</th><th>Schedule</th><th>Units</th><th>Action</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {available.map((c) => {
                      const reason = blockReason(c);
                      return (
                        <tr key={c.id}>
                          <td><strong>{c.subject.code}</strong><br /><small className="muted">{c.subject.name}</small></td>
                          <td>{c.section}</td>
                          <td>{c.teacher?.name}</td>
                          <td>{c.day} {timeRange(c)}<br /><small className="muted">{c.room}</small></td>
                          <td>{c.subject.units}</td>
                          <td>
                            <button className="btn-primary btn-sm" disabled={!!reason} onClick={() => register(c)}>Register</button>
                            {reason && <small className="muted block">{reason}</small>}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
                {available.length === 0 && <EmptyState text="No other classes are open right now." />}
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        );

      case 'schedule': {
        const starts = [...new Set(active.map((r) => r.cls.start))].sort((a, b) => toMin(a) - toMin(b));
        return (
          <>
            <PageHeader title="Class Schedule" subtitle="Dashed blocks are waiting for approval.">
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => window.print()}><Printer size={16} /> Print</button>
            </PageHeader>

            {conflicts.length > 0 && (
              <div className="alert alert-danger" role="alert">
                <AlertTriangle size={18} />
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <strong>{conflicts.length} schedule conflict(s).</strong> Drop one subject in each pair to fix it.
                  {conflicts.map(([a, b]) => (
                    <div key={a.id + b.id} className="conflict-row">
                      <span>{a.cls.subject.code} and {b.cls.subject.code} overlap on {a.cls.day}.</span>
                      <span className="no-print">
                        <button className="btn-danger btn-sm" onClick={() => setDropTarget(a)}>Drop {a.cls.subject.code}</button>{' '}
                        <button className="btn-danger btn-sm" onClick={() => setDropTarget(b)}>Drop {b.cls.subject.code}</button>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '1.5rem' }}>
              {active.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState text="Your schedule is empty. Register for classes to see them here." />
              ) : (
                <div className="table-scroll">
                  <table className="schedule-table">
                    <thead><tr><th>Time</th>{DAYS.map((d) => <th key={d}>{d}</th>)}</tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {starts.map((s) => (
                        <tr key={s}>
                          <td><strong>{fmtTime(s)}</strong></td>
                          {DAYS.map((d) => {
                            const r = active.find((x) => x.cls.day === d && x.cls.start === s);
                            return (
                              <td key={d}>
                                {r && (
                                  <div className={`schedule-block ${r.status === 'pending' ? 'pending' : ''} ${conflictIds.has(r.id) ? 'conflict' : ''}`}>
                                    <strong>{r.cls.subject.code}</strong><br />
                                    {r.cls.subject.name}<br />
                                    <small>{timeRange(r.cls)} - {r.cls.room}</small>
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </td>
                            );
                          })}
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        );
      }

      case 'records':
        return (
          <>
            <PageHeader title="Academic Records" subtitle="Grades use a 1.00 (best) to 5.00 scale. 3.00 or better is passing.">
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => window.print()}><Printer size={16} /> Print</button>
            </PageHeader>
            <div className="table-container">
              <div className="table-scroll">
                <table>
                  <thead><tr><th>Subject</th><th>Units</th><th>Midterm Grade</th><th>Finals Grade</th><th>Final Rating</th><th>Remarks</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {rated.map(({ r, rating }) => {
                      const g = gradeOf(db.grades, student.id, r.cls.subjectId);
                      const show = (v) => (v === '' || v == null ? '—' : Number(v).toFixed(2));
                      const remark = remarkOf(rating);
                      return (
                        <tr key={r.id}>
                          <td>{r.cls.subject.name}<br /><small className="muted">{r.cls.subject.code}</small></td>
                          <td>{r.cls.subject.units}</td>
                          <td>{show(g?.midterm)}</td>
                          <td>{show(g?.finals)}</td>
                          <td><strong>{show(rating)}</strong></td>
                          <td>{remark ? <Badge status={remark} /> : <span className="muted">Not posted</span>}</td>
                        </tr>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                  {gwa != null && (
                    <tfoot>
                      <tr><td colSpan="4"><strong>General Weighted Average (GWA)</strong></td><td colSpan="2"><strong>{gwa.toFixed(2)}</strong></td></tr>
                    </tfoot>
                  )}
                </table>
                {rated.length === 0 && <EmptyState text="No records yet. Grades appear here after your registrations are approved." />}
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        );

      default:
        return <div>Page not found</div>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <Shell
      brand="AttendX Student" userLabel={student.name} sections={[{ items: NAV.map((n) => (n.key === 'subjects' ? { ...n, count: pending.length } : n)) }]}
      active={page} onNav={setPage} theme={theme} toggleTheme={toggleTheme} onLogout={onLogout}
    >
      {renderContent()}

      {dropTarget && (
        <ConfirmModal
          title={dropTarget.status === 'rejected' ? 'Remove Subject' : 'Drop Subject'}
          confirmLabel={dropTarget.status === 'rejected' ? 'Remove' : 'Drop'}
          message={<p>Do you want to {dropTarget.status === 'rejected' ? 'remove' : 'drop'} <strong>{dropTarget.cls.subject.code} - {dropTarget.cls.subject.name}</strong>? You can register again later.</p>}
          onConfirm={confirmDrop}
          onClose={() => setDropTarget(null)}
        />
      )}
      {profileModal === 'edit' && <EditProfileModal student={student} db={db} notify={notify} onClose={() => setProfileModal(null)} />}
      {profileModal === 'password' && <PasswordModal student={student} db={db} notify={notify} onClose={() => setProfileModal(null)} />}
    </Shell>
  );
}

// ---------- small pieces ----------
const Progress = ({ label, value, width, fill }) => (
  <div className="progress-group">
    <div className="progress-label"><span>{label}</span><span>{value}</span></div>
    <div className="progress-bar"><div className={`progress-fill ${fill}`} style={{ width: `${width}%` }} /></div>
  </div>
);

const Info = ({ label, value }) => (
  <div>
    <p className="muted" style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', marginBottom: '0.25rem' }}>{label}</p>
    <p style={{ fontWeight: 500 }}>{value}</p>
  </div>
);

function EditProfileModal({ student, db, notify, onClose }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: student.name, phone: student.phone || '', dob: student.dob || '' });
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const fields = [
    { name: 'name', label: 'Full Name' },
    { name: 'phone', label: 'Phone', optional: true, placeholder: '+639171234567' },
    { name: 'dob', label: 'Date of Birth', type: 'date', optional: true },
  ];
  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!form.name.trim()) return setError('Name is required.');
    if (form.phone && !/^\+?[0-9 -]{7,15}$/.test(form.phone)) return setError('Phone must have 7 to 15 digits.');
    if (form.dob && new Date(form.dob) > new Date()) return setError('Date of birth must be a past date.');
    db.editStudent(student.id, { name: form.name.trim(), phone: form.phone.trim(), dob: form.dob });
    notify('Profile updated.');
    onClose();
  };
  return (
    <Modal title="Edit Profile" onClose={onClose}>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        {error && <div className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
        {fields.map((f) => <Field key={f.name} field={f} value={form[f.name]} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })} />)}
        <p className="form-hint">Email, course and student number can only be changed by the admin.</p>
        <div className="modal-actions">
          <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save Changes</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function PasswordModal({ student, db, notify, onClose }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ current: '', next: '', confirm: '' });
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const fields = [
    { name: 'current', label: 'Current Password', type: 'password', autoComplete: 'current-password' },
    { name: 'next', label: 'New Password', type: 'password', autoComplete: 'new-password', hint: 'At least 8 characters.' },
    { name: 'confirm', label: 'Confirm New Password', type: 'password', autoComplete: 'new-password' },
  ];
  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (form.current !== student.password) return setError('Current password is wrong.');
    if (form.next.length < 8) return setError('New password must have at least 8 characters.');
    if (form.next === form.current) return setError('New password must be different from the current one.');
    if (form.next !== form.confirm) return setError('New passwords do not match.');
    db.editStudent(student.id, { password: form.next });
    notify('Password changed.');
    onClose();
  };
  return (
    <Modal title="Change Password" onClose={onClose}>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        {error && <div className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
        {fields.map((f) => <Field key={f.name} field={f} value={form[f.name]} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })} />)}
        <div className="modal-actions">
          <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
          <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Change Password</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/src/AdminPortal.jsx">
import { useState } from 'react';
import {
  LayoutDashboard, Users, BookOpen, Book, GraduationCap, ClipboardCheck, Award, Check, X, Edit2, Search,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Shell, PageHeader, Badge, Modal, Field, EmptyState } from './components';
import CrudPage from './CrudPage';
import {
  COURSES, DAYS, ADMIN, toMin, timeRange, overlaps, fmtDate, gradeOf, finalRating, remarkOf, pct,
} from './data';

const EMAIL_RE = /^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/;
const PHONE_RE = /^\+?[0-9 -]{7,15}$/;
const opts = (list, label) => list.map((x) => ({ value: x.id, label: label(x) }));

export default function AdminPortal({ db, theme, toggleTheme, onLogout, notify }) {
  const [page, setPage] = useState('dashboard');

  const subj = (id) => db.subjects.find((s) => s.id === id);
  const teacher = (id) => db.teachers.find((t) => t.id === id);
  const student = (id) => db.students.find((s) => s.id === id);
  const cls = (id) => db.classes.find((c) => c.id === id);
  const enrolled = (classId) => db.registrations.filter((r) => r.classId === classId && r.status === 'approved').length;
  const pending = db.registrations.filter((r) => r.status === 'pending');
  const classTitle = (c) => `${subj(c.subjectId)?.code} (${c.section})`;

  const sections = [
    { items: [{ key: 'dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard }] },
    {
      label: 'Academics',
      items: [
        { key: 'classes', label: 'Classes', icon: BookOpen },
        { key: 'teachers', label: 'Teachers', icon: Users },
        { key: 'students', label: 'Students', icon: GraduationCap },
        { key: 'subjects', label: 'Subjects', icon: Book },
      ],
    },
    {
      label: 'Enrollment',
      items: [
        { key: 'registrations', label: 'Registrations', icon: ClipboardCheck, count: pending.length },
        { key: 'grades', label: 'Grades', icon: Award },
      ],
    },
  ];
  const title = sections.flatMap((s) => s.items).find((i) => i.key === page).label;

  // ----- Registration actions -----
  const approve = (r) => {
    const target = cls(r.classId);
    const clash = db.registrations
      .filter((x) => x.studentId === r.studentId && x.status === 'approved' && x.id !== r.id)
      .map((x) => cls(x.classId))
      .find((c) => c && overlaps(c, target));
    if (clash) return notify(`Cannot approve. It overlaps with ${subj(clash.subjectId)?.code} on ${clash.day}.`, 'error');
    db.setRegStatus(r.id, { status: 'approved' });
    notify('Registration approved.');
  };
  const reject = (r) => {
    db.setRegStatus(r.id, { status: 'rejected' });
    notify('Registration rejected.');
  };

  // ----- Validation -----
  const validateStudent = (v, row, batch = []) => {
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(v.email)) return 'Enter a valid email address.';
    if ([...db.students, ...batch].some((s) => s.id !== row?.id && s.email.toLowerCase() === v.email.toLowerCase()))
      return 'That email is already used by another student.';
    if (v.phone && !PHONE_RE.test(v.phone)) return 'Phone must have 7 to 15 digits.';
    if (v.dob && (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v.dob) || new Date(v.dob) > new Date()))
      return 'Date of birth must be a past date (YYYY-MM-DD).';
    if (!COURSES.includes(v.course)) return 'Pick a course from the list.';
    return null;
  };

  const validateTeacher = (v, row, batch = []) => {
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(v.email)) return 'Enter a valid email address.';
    if ([...db.teachers, ...batch].some((t) => t.id !== row?.id && t.email.toLowerCase() === v.email.toLowerCase()))
      return 'That email is already used by another teacher.';
    return null;
  };

  const validateSubject = (v, row, batch = []) => {
    if ([...db.subjects, ...batch].some((s) => s.id !== row?.id && s.code.toLowerCase() === v.code.toLowerCase()))
      return 'That subject code already exists.';
    if (!Number.isInteger(v.units) || v.units < 1 || v.units > 6) return 'Units must be a whole number from 1 to 6.';
    return null;
  };

  const validateClass = (v, row) => {
    if (toMin(v.end) <= toMin(v.start)) return 'End time must be after the start time.';
    const cand = { ...v, id: row?.id };
    for (const c of db.classes) {
      if (c.id === cand.id || !overlaps(c, cand)) continue;
      const when = `${c.day} ${timeRange(c)}`;
      if (c.teacherId === v.teacherId) return `Teacher conflict: ${teacher(c.teacherId)?.name} already teaches ${classTitle(c)} on ${when}.`;
      if (c.room.toLowerCase() === v.room.toLowerCase()) return `Room conflict: ${c.room} is already used by ${classTitle(c)} on ${when}.`;
      if (c.section.toLowerCase() === v.section.toLowerCase()) return `Section conflict: ${v.section} already has ${classTitle(c)} on ${when}.`;
    }
    return null;
  };

  // ----- Pages -----
  const renderPage = () => {
    switch (page) {
      case 'dashboard': {
        const perDay = DAYS.map((d) => ({ d, n: db.classes.filter((c) => c.day === d).length }));
        const max = Math.max(1, ...perDay.map((x) => x.n));
        return (
          <>
            <div className="stats-grid">
              <div className="stat-card"><p className="stat-label">Students</p><div className="stat-value">{db.students.length}</div></div>
              <div className="stat-card"><p className="stat-label">Teachers</p><div className="stat-value">{db.teachers.length}</div></div>
              <div className="stat-card"><p className="stat-label">Classes</p><div className="stat-value">{db.classes.length}</div></div>
              <div className="stat-card clickable" role="button" tabIndex={0} onClick={() => setPage('registrations')} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && setPage('registrations')}>
                <p className="stat-label">Pending Registrations</p>
                <div className="stat-value">
                  {pending.length}
                  <Badge status={pending.length ? 'pending' : 'approved'}>{pending.length ? 'Review now' : 'All clear'}</Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="dashboard-columns">
              <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '1.5rem' }}>
                <div className="card-title-row">
                  <h3>Pending Registrations</h3>
                  <a href="#" className="link-accent" onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPage('registrations'); }}>View all</a>
                </div>
                {pending.length === 0 ? (
                  <EmptyState text="No registrations are waiting for approval." />
                ) : (
                  <div className="table-scroll">
                    <table>
                      <thead><tr><th>Student</th><th>Class</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {pending.slice(0, 5).map((r) => (
                          <tr key={r.id}>
                            <td>{student(r.studentId)?.name}</td>
                            <td>{classTitle(cls(r.classId))}</td>
                            <td>
                              <div className="actions-cell">
                                <button className="action-edit" title="Approve" aria-label="Approve" onClick={() => approve(r)}><Check size={16} /></button>
                                <button className="action-delete" title="Reject" aria-label="Reject" onClick={() => reject(r)}><X size={16} /></button>
                              </div>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="table-container" style={{ padding: '1.5rem' }}>
                <h3 style={{ marginBottom: '1.5rem' }}>Classes per Day</h3>
                {perDay.map(({ d, n }) => (
                  <div className="progress-group" key={d}>
                    <div className="progress-label"><span>{d}</span><span>{n}</span></div>
                    <div className="progress-bar"><div className="progress-fill fill-green" style={{ width: `${pct(n, max)}%` }} /></div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        );
      }

      case 'classes':
        return (
          <CrudPage
            noun="Class" notify={notify} rows={db.classes}
            nameOf={(c) => classTitle(c)}
            searchText={(c) => `${subj(c.subjectId)?.code} ${subj(c.subjectId)?.name} ${teacher(c.teacherId)?.name} ${c.section} ${c.room} ${c.day}`}
            fields={[
              { name: 'subjectId', label: 'Subject', type: 'select', options: opts(db.subjects, (s) => `${s.code} - ${s.name}`) },
              { name: 'teacherId', label: 'Teacher', type: 'select', options: opts(db.teachers, (t) => t.name) },
              { name: 'section', label: 'Section', placeholder: 'BSIT-3A' },
              { name: 'day', label: 'Day', type: 'select', options: DAYS.map((d) => ({ value: d, label: d })) },
              { name: 'start', label: 'Start Time', type: 'time' },
              { name: 'end', label: 'End Time', type: 'time' },
              { name: 'room', label: 'Room', placeholder: 'Room 301' },
            ]}
            columns={[
              { label: 'Subject', render: (c) => <><strong>{subj(c.subjectId)?.code}</strong><br /><small className="muted">{subj(c.subjectId)?.name}</small></> },
              { label: 'Teacher', render: (c) => teacher(c.teacherId)?.name },
              { label: 'Section', render: (c) => c.section },
              { label: 'Day', render: (c) => c.day },
              { label: 'Time', render: (c) => timeRange(c) },
              { label: 'Room', render: (c) => c.room },
              { label: 'Enrolled', render: (c) => enrolled(c.id) },
            ]}
            validate={validateClass}
            onAdd={db.addClass}
            onEdit={db.editClass}
            onDelete={db.removeClass}
            deleteNote={(c) => (enrolled(c.id) ? `${enrolled(c.id)} enrolled student(s) will lose this class.` : '')}
          />
        );

      case 'teachers':
        return (
          <CrudPage
            noun="Teacher" notify={notify} rows={db.teachers}
            searchText={(t) => `${t.name} ${t.email} ${t.department}`}
            fields={[
              { name: 'name', label: 'Full Name', placeholder: 'Prof. Reyes' },
              { name: 'email', label: 'School Email', type: 'email' },
              { name: 'department', label: 'Department' },
            ]}
            columns={[
              { label: 'Name', render: (t) => t.name },
              { label: 'Email', render: (t) => t.email },
              { label: 'Department', render: (t) => t.department },
              { label: 'Classes', render: (t) => db.classes.filter((c) => c.teacherId === t.id).length },
            ]}
            validate={validateTeacher}
            onAdd={db.addTeacher}
            onEdit={db.editTeacher}
            onDelete={db.removeTeacher}
            canDelete={(t) => {
              const n = db.classes.filter((c) => c.teacherId === t.id).length;
              return n ? `${t.name} still teaches ${n} class(es). Reassign or delete those classes first.` : null;
            }}
            bulk={{ fields: ['name', 'email', 'department'], hint: 'Full Name, Email, Department', onImport: db.addTeachers }}
          />
        );

      case 'students':
        return (
          <CrudPage
            noun="Student" notify={notify} rows={db.students}
            searchText={(s) => `${s.name} ${s.studentNo} ${s.email} ${s.phone} ${s.course}`}
            fields={[
              { name: 'name', label: 'Full Name' },
              { name: 'email', label: 'School Email', type: 'email' },
              { name: 'phone', label: 'Phone', optional: true, placeholder: '+639171234567' },
              { name: 'dob', label: 'Date of Birth', type: 'date', optional: true },
              { name: 'course', label: 'Course', type: 'select', default: COURSES[0], options: COURSES.map((c) => ({ value: c, label: c })) },
            ]}
            columns={[
              { label: 'Student No.', render: (s) => s.studentNo },
              { label: 'Name', render: (s) => s.name },
              { label: 'Email', render: (s) => s.email },
              { label: 'Phone', render: (s) => s.phone || '—' },
              { label: 'Date of Birth', render: (s) => fmtDate(s.dob) },
              { label: 'Course', render: (s) => s.course },
            ]}
            validate={validateStudent}
            onAdd={db.addStudent}
            onEdit={db.editStudent}
            onDelete={db.removeStudent}
            deleteNote={(s) => {
              const n = db.registrations.filter((r) => r.studentId === s.id).length;
              return n ? `Their ${n} registration(s) and grades will be removed too.` : '';
            }}
            bulk={{
              fields: ['name', 'email', 'phone', 'dob'],
              hint: 'Full Name, Email, Phone, Date of Birth (YYYY-MM-DD)',
              note: 'Student numbers are created automatically. The default password is Welcome123.',
              defaults: { course: COURSES[0] },
              onImport: db.addStudents,
            }}
          />
        );

      case 'subjects':
        return (
          <CrudPage
            noun="Subject" notify={notify} rows={db.subjects}
            nameOf={(s) => `${s.code} - ${s.name}`}
            searchText={(s) => `${s.code} ${s.name}`}
            fields={[
              { name: 'code', label: 'Subject Code', placeholder: 'IT205' },
              { name: 'name', label: 'Subject Name' },
              { name: 'units', label: 'Units', type: 'number', min: 1, max: 6, step: 1, default: 3 },
            ]}
            columns={[
              { label: 'Code', render: (s) => <strong>{s.code}</strong> },
              { label: 'Name', render: (s) => s.name },
              { label: 'Units', render: (s) => s.units },
              { label: 'Classes', render: (s) => db.classes.filter((c) => c.subjectId === s.id).length },
            ]}
            validate={validateSubject}
            onAdd={(v) => db.addSubject({ ...v, code: v.code.toUpperCase() })}
            onEdit={(id, v) => db.editSubject(id, { ...v, code: v.code.toUpperCase() })}
            onDelete={db.removeSubject}
            deleteNote={(s) => {
              const n = db.classes.filter((c) => c.subjectId === s.id).length;
              return n ? `Its ${n} class(es), registrations and grades will be removed too.` : '';
            }}
            bulk={{
              fields: ['code', 'name', 'units'],
              hint: 'Code, Name, Units',
              onImport: (list) => db.addSubjects(list.map((s) => ({ ...s, code: s.code.toUpperCase() }))),
            }}
          />
        );

      case 'registrations':
        return <RegistrationsPage db={db} student={student} cls={cls} subj={subj} classTitle={classTitle} approve={approve} reject={reject} />;

      case 'grades':
        return <GradesPage db={db} notify={notify} />;

      default:
        return <div>Page not found</div>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <Shell
      brand="AttendX Admin" userLabel={ADMIN.name} sections={sections} active={page} onNav={setPage}
      theme={theme} toggleTheme={toggleTheme} onLogout={onLogout}
    >
      <PageHeader title={title} />
      {renderPage()}
    </Shell>
  );
}

// ---------------- Registrations ----------------
function RegistrationsPage({ db, student, cls, subj, classTitle, approve, reject }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('pending');
  const count = (s) => db.registrations.filter((r) => s === 'all' || r.status === s).length;
  const rows = db.registrations.filter((r) => tab === 'all' || r.status === tab);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="tabs no-print">
        {['pending', 'approved', 'rejected', 'all'].map((t) => (
          <button key={t} className={`tab ${tab === t ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab(t)}>
            {t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)} ({count(t)})
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="table-container">
        <div className="table-scroll">
          <table>
            <thead><tr><th>Student</th><th>Subject</th><th>Class</th><th>Schedule</th><th>Status</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((r) => {
                const c = cls(r.classId);
                return (
                  <tr key={r.id}>
                    <td>{student(r.studentId)?.name}<br /><small className="muted">{student(r.studentId)?.studentNo}</small></td>
                    <td>{subj(c.subjectId)?.name}</td>
                    <td>{classTitle(c)}</td>
                    <td>{c.day} {timeRange(c)}</td>
                    <td><Badge status={r.status} /></td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="actions-cell">
                        {r.status !== 'approved' && <button className="action-edit" title="Approve" aria-label="Approve" onClick={() => approve(r)}><Check size={16} /></button>}
                        {r.status !== 'rejected' && <button className="action-delete" title="Reject" aria-label="Reject" onClick={() => reject(r)}><X size={16} /></button>}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          {rows.length === 0 && <EmptyState text={`No ${tab === 'all' ? '' : tab + ' '}registrations.`} />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

// ---------------- Grades ----------------
function GradesPage({ db, notify }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ midterm: '', finals: '' });
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const seen = new Set();
  const rows = [];
  db.registrations.filter((r) => r.status === 'approved').forEach((r) => {
    const c = db.classes.find((x) => x.id === r.classId);
    if (!c || seen.has(`${r.studentId}|${c.subjectId}`)) return;
    seen.add(`${r.studentId}|${c.subjectId}`);
    rows.push({
      key: `${r.studentId}|${c.subjectId}`,
      student: db.students.find((s) => s.id === r.studentId),
      subject: db.subjects.find((s) => s.id === c.subjectId),
      grade: gradeOf(db.grades, r.studentId, c.subjectId),
    });
  });

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const shown = rows.filter((x) => !q || `${x.student?.name} ${x.subject?.code} ${x.subject?.name}`.toLowerCase().includes(q));

  const fields = [
    { name: 'midterm', label: 'Midterm Grade', type: 'number', min: 1, max: 5, step: 0.25, optional: true, hint: '1.00 is the best. 5.00 is the lowest.' },
    { name: 'finals', label: 'Finals Grade', type: 'number', min: 1, max: 5, step: 0.25, optional: true },
  ];

  const openEdit = (x) => {
    setError('');
    setForm({ midterm: x.grade?.midterm ?? '', finals: x.grade?.finals ?? '' });
    setEditing(x);
  };

  const save = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const vals = ['midterm', 'finals'].map((k) => (form[k] === '' ? '' : Number(form[k])));
    if (vals.some((v) => v !== '' && (Number.isNaN(v) || v < 1 || v > 5))) return setError('Grades must be from 1.00 to 5.00.');
    db.saveGrade(editing.student.id, editing.subject.id, vals[0], vals[1]);
    notify('Grades saved.');
    setEditing(null);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar no-print">
        <div className="search-bar">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Search student or subject..." aria-label="Search grades" value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
          <div className="btn-icon"><Search size={18} /></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="table-container">
        <div className="table-scroll">
          <table>
            <thead><tr><th>Student</th><th>Subject</th><th>Midterm</th><th>Finals</th><th>Final Rating</th><th>Remarks</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {shown.map((x) => {
                const rating = finalRating(x.grade);
                const remark = remarkOf(rating);
                return (
                  <tr key={x.key}>
                    <td>{x.student?.name}</td>
                    <td>{x.subject?.code} <small className="muted">{x.subject?.name}</small></td>
                    <td>{x.grade?.midterm === '' || x.grade == null ? '—' : Number(x.grade.midterm).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td>{x.grade?.finals === '' || x.grade == null ? '—' : Number(x.grade.finals).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td><strong>{rating == null ? '—' : rating.toFixed(2)}</strong></td>
                    <td>{remark ? <Badge status={remark} /> : '—'}</td>
                    <td><div className="actions-cell"><button className="action-edit" title="Edit grades" aria-label="Edit grades" onClick={() => openEdit(x)}><Edit2 size={16} /></button></div></td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          {shown.length === 0 && <EmptyState text="No approved registrations yet. Approve a registration to enter grades." />}
        </div>
      </div>

      {editing && (
        <Modal title={`Grades: ${editing.student.name}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
          <form onSubmit={save}>
            <p style={{ marginBottom: '1rem' }}>{editing.subject.code} - {editing.subject.name}</p>
            {error && <div className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
            {fields.map((f) => <Field key={f.name} field={f} value={form[f.name]} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })} />)}
            <div className="modal-actions">
              <button type="button" className="btn-cancel" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</button>
              <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save Grades</button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
</content>

<content full_path="student-hub/client/src/store.js">
import { useState } from 'react';
import {
  uid, nextStudentNo, DEFAULT_PASSWORD,
  SEED_TEACHERS, SEED_SUBJECTS, SEED_CLASSES, SEED_STUDENTS, SEED_REGISTRATIONS, SEED_GRADES,
} from './data';

// All data lives in React state for now. Later, swap these actions for API calls.
export function useDB() {
  const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState(SEED_TEACHERS);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState(SEED_SUBJECTS);
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState(SEED_CLASSES);
  const [students, setStudents] = useState(SEED_STUDENTS);
  const [registrations, setRegistrations] = useState(SEED_REGISTRATIONS);
  const [grades, setGrades] = useState(SEED_GRADES);

  const add = (set) => (item) => set((l) => [...l, { ...item, id: uid() }]);
  const edit = (set) => (id, patch) => set((l) => l.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)));
  const del = (set) => (id) => set((l) => l.filter((x) => x.id !== id));

  const addStudents = (items) =>
    setStudents((l) => {
      const list = [...l];
      items.forEach((i) =>
        list.push({ ...i, id: uid(), studentNo: nextStudentNo(list), password: i.password || DEFAULT_PASSWORD })
      );
      return list;
    });

  return {
    teachers, subjects, classes, students, registrations, grades,

    // Teachers
    addTeacher: add(setTeachers),
    editTeacher: edit(setTeachers),
    removeTeacher: del(setTeachers),
    addTeachers: (items) => setTeachers((l) => [...l, ...items.map((i) => ({ ...i, id: uid() }))]),

    // Subjects (deleting one also removes its classes, registrations and grades)
    addSubject: add(setSubjects),
    editSubject: edit(setSubjects),
    addSubjects: (items) => setSubjects((l) => [...l, ...items.map((i) => ({ ...i, id: uid() }))]),
    removeSubject: (id) => {
      const classIds = classes.filter((c) => c.subjectId === id).map((c) => c.id);
      setSubjects((l) => l.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
      setClasses((l) => l.filter((c) => c.subjectId !== id));
      setRegistrations((l) => l.filter((r) => !classIds.includes(r.classId)));
      setGrades((l) => l.filter((g) => g.subjectId !== id));
    },

    // Classes (deleting one also removes its registrations)
    addClass: add(setClasses),
    editClass: edit(setClasses),
    removeClass: (id) => {
      setClasses((l) => l.filter((c) => c.id !== id));
      setRegistrations((l) => l.filter((r) => r.classId !== id));
    },

    // Students (deleting one also removes their registrations and grades)
    addStudents,
    addStudent: (item) => addStudents([item]),
    editStudent: edit(setStudents),
    removeStudent: (id) => {
      setStudents((l) => l.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
      setRegistrations((l) => l.filter((r) => r.studentId !== id));
      setGrades((l) => l.filter((g) => g.studentId !== id));
    },

    // Registrations
    register: (studentId, classId) =>
      setRegistrations((l) => [...l, { id: uid(), studentId, classId, status: 'pending' }]),
    setRegStatus: edit(setRegistrations),
    dropReg: del(setRegistrations),

    // Grades
    saveGrade: (studentId, subjectId, midterm, finals) =>
      setGrades((l) =>
        l.some((g) => g.studentId === studentId && g.subjectId === subjectId)
          ? l.map((g) => (g.studentId === studentId && g.subjectId === subjectId ? { ...g, midterm, finals } : g))
          : [...l, { id: uid(), studentId, subjectId, midterm, finals }]
      ),
  };
}
</content>
